# PLAN information-architecture preservation mapping — 2026-11-16

Immutable cutover account, **not a planning source**. Root [PLAN.md](../../PLAN.md) selects work. Fixed source PLAN@6c7e24c3161fe80dc4c5bd6bfe1bec5cd04fd8dc; 3,433 lines / 227,054 bytes / 3,012 nonblank occurrences. [History](plan-history-2026-11-16-6c7e24c3.md) has exactly ten banner lines; original line n is history line n+10. Body SHA-256 ec43ea501cd160302a328467858b0c09b3945e680dca5b96fbcd17d493b961eb. No original byte or NUL was rewritten.

All 112 history units retain the coordinator-reviewed classification; no still-binding condition was discovered there. Per-unit bases remain specific to their source/decision/commit. The revision keeps surviving conditions and dependent scope verbatim; superseded intermediate states and ended step narratives stay in history. A later delivery never retrospectively proves approval or checks.

## Sentence lookup rule

`Mnnnn.Sxx` numbers sentences/fragments in source order within that original coherent unit. Markdown heading/table rows and incomplete labels are atomic fragments. Checkbox/list prefixes are structural; internal spelling, punctuation, whitespace and wrapped lines are unchanged. The full-quote inventory and every kept/omitted sentence remain in `E:/g315/codex-tmp/g/sentence_audit.json`; the repo table stores IDs rather than duplicating quotes. Adjacent kept sentences preserve their original intervening whitespace. Neutral root labels state scope/status only.

## Preservation and evidence boundaries

Machine checks use temporary scripts, not repository policy/tools. Required checks: exact banner/body digest and occurrence-aware source coverage; all 400 non-history units to root clauses; every authority-index H3 scope/limits; pure header/state/summary readers including every constraint; baseline exact H2 inventory; focused PLAN-related tests and git diff --check. Results and measured artifact sizes are retained in the delivery evidence outside the repo. These checks are not independent semantic approval or proof of framework correctness.

The #311 follow-up cell is —, meaning **no follow-up recorded**, correcting the step-1 console-rendering interpretation. The two full-quote step-1 documents remain under E:/g315/codex-tmp/g/; this compact record intentionally does not repeat their quotations.

Compatibility discovery: the requested suite also reads real PLAN counts in `tests/test_plan_parser.py:50`.
